Let's derive delta exact thoroughly.

Let h old binary. Let L = 2n-1, indices t from -(n-1) to n-1 (mapped idx=t+n-1).
q[t] = sum_{i=0}^{n-1} h_i * (1 - h_{i+t}) for t such that i+t in [0,n-1]. Equivalent for t>=0: q[t]=sum_{i=0}^{n-1-t} h_i(1-h_{i+t}); t<0: sum_{i=-t}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i+t}).
Define s_i = h_i. For swap: new h' at a 0, b 1.
For each t, change in first factor terms where i=a and second factor valid; change in second factor where i+t=a; change where i=b; where i+t=b. More systematic:
q = ∑_{p: p+t valid} h_p (1 - h_{p+t}).
Change due to h_p factor: if p=a, old term 1*(1-h_{a+t}) if valid, new 0; change - (1-h_{a+t})old with valid. if p=b, old 0, new 1*(1-h'_{b+t}), change + (1-h'_{b+t}). But if b+t=a, h'_{a}=0, h old a=1, old value h old a? The new factor uses h' so need account. Similarly change due to second factor: if p+t=a (p=a-t), old term h_p*1, new h'_p*0; change -h'_p (or old? term old h_old_p*1, new h'_p*0 = -h'_p). If p=b? We'll handle via factors with new values maybe easier.

Alternative compute new q[t] from scratch in O(1)? Since q[t] for all t can be updated by considering all pairs involving a or b in new h. Number of affected pairs O(n). We can compute new q = old q - old contributions of affected pairs + new contributions. Affected pairs are all (p,p+t) where p=a or p+t=a or p=b or p+t=b for a given t. For each t, at most 4 ordered pairs; contribution of pair (u,v) with v-u=t: h_u(1-h_v) (where u first). We can compute old contribution and new contribution by evaluating old h with modifications. This avoids cross special? Let's define for any t, set of first indices P = {a, a-t, b, b-t} intersect [0,n-1] such that first+ t valid? Actually for pair first p, second p+t valid. p in A_t = [0,n-1] and p+t in [0,n-1]. For given t, valid p range: if t>=0: 0..n-1-t; if t<0: -t..n-1. Affected p include a if valid; a-t if valid; b if valid; b-t if valid. For each p in affected set unique, compute old contrib = oldh[p]*(1-oldh[p+t]); new contrib with h' (only a,b changed). dq = sum(new-old). Since affected pairs may overlap (e.g., p=a and b-t same), we need unique set to not double. This is exact and O(number affected) per t, could be up to 4t. We can vectorize over t by computing for p=a,a-t,b,b-t contributions simultaneously? For each p expression as function of t:
For p=a: valid t range such that a+t in [0,n-1]: t ∈ [-a, n-1-a]. Contribution old = 1*(1-h[a+t]) (if a+t != a? t could 0? q0=0 because 1-ha=0; fine), new=0 (since p a becomes 0) => delta = -old? If p+t=b? old h[b]=0 new h[b]=1; old contrib=1*(1-0)=1, new=0 -> delta -1. Formula new h[p]=0, regardless neighbor -> 0. old uses oldh[p+t]. So delta = - oldh? precisely old contrib = 1 - oldh[p+t]; delta= -(1 - oldh[p+t]) (including if p+t out? valid only).
For p=b: new h[b]=1, old=0. delta = new contrib = 1*(1 - newh[b+t]) (if valid). Need newh at neighbor: if b+t=a =>0 else oldh.
For p=a-t (so second is a): old contrib = oldh[p]*1 (if valid p and t); newh[p]=0 if p=a? p cannot equal a unless t=0. p may equal b if a-t=b (t=a-b). New contrib = newh[p]*0 =0. delta = - oldh[p]? Function make_delta(h,a,b,n): returns dq int32. But we can inline in loop. We need avoid function call overhead? okay.
Pseudo:
qq = q
L=2*n-1
def delta_swap(q, h, a, b, n):
    dq = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int32)
    # first a
    start = n - 1 - a
    dq[start:start+n] += h.astype(int32?) - 1 # h uint8; need int32 view to avoid uint wrap
But h is uint8; h -1 gives uint8 wrap. Need h_int = h.astype(np.int32) once, maintain? But h changes with swaps; update h[a]=0,h[b]=1; h_int update. Use int32 h.
    h_arr int32.
    dq[start:start+n] += h_arr - 1
    # first b
    start = n - 1 - b
    seg = 1 - h_arr
    dq[start:start+n] += seg
    j = (start + a) # because j index into neighbor j=a; index idx=start+j
    dq[idx_a] += 1
    idx_b = start + b
    dq[idx_b] -= 1
    # second a
    # slice dq[a:a+n][::-1] += h_arr
    dq[a:a+n][::-1] += h_arr
    dq[n-1] -= 1
    dq[n-1 + a - b] += 1
    # second b
    dq[b:b+n][::-1] -= h_arr
    # special corrections
    dq[n-1 + b - a] += 1
    dq[n-1 + a - b] -= 1
    return dq

  m=f.max(); alpha; sh=alpha*(f-m); shmax=sh.max(); p=np.exp(sh-shmax); p/=p.sum()

  thresh=max(1e-6, 1/ (2*n*n)?) maybe 1e-5.
  idx = np.where(p>1e-5)[0]
  gh=np.zeros(n)
  for idx in idx:
    t=idx-(n-1); w=float(p[idx])
    # add gradient
    if t==0:
      gh[:]+=w*(1 - 2*h)?? A indicator all ones w, derivative -2w h. But A0=sum h, gradient 1. Yes gh += w; gh -= 2w*h.
    elif t>0:

      gh[:L] += w
      gh[:L] -= w*h[t:]
      gh[t:] -= w*h[:L]
    else:

      gh[s:] += w
      gh[s:] -= w*h[:L]
      gh[:L] -= w*h[s:]

  return gx

Implement binary greedy_improve(h_int,q,ones,zeros,dl):
  curmax=q.max()
  improved_any=False
  Loop until deadline or no improve:
    active = indices where q >= curmax-1
    score ones/zeros as above length n int32 zero.
    for k in active:
      t = k-(n-1)
      if t==0: continue
      if t>0:
        L=n-t
        # contribution matrix c = h[:L]*(1-h[t:])
        # These c[i]=1 if one i and zero i+t.

        score_zeros[t:] += c

    ones_score=score_ones[ones]; zeros_score=score_zeros[zeros]
    # top positions: argsort descending, take up to 30 or all if small
    top_a = ones[np.argsort(ones_score)[-30:]] but reverse? scores length N. Use np.argsort(-scores)

Let's further analyze binary local search to maybe find qmax 47/48. Parent likely found 47.6 continuous. We can implement a "best-first" binary descent that might find qmax 47. Let's design it robustly.

Binary best-first algorithm details:
Input h binary length n even.
q exact int, curmax.
Loop:
  if time out break.
  active = np.where(q >= curmax - 1)[0] (or curmax-2)
  Compute scores for ones/zeros:
    score_ones=zeros(n,int32), score_zeros=zeros(n,int32)
    for k in active:
      t=k-(n-1)
      if t==0: continue
      if t>0:
        L=n-t
        c = h[:L].astype(int32) * (1 - h[t:].astype(int32))  # 1 where q_t contributes
        score_ones[:L] += c
        score_zeros[t:] += c
      else:
        s=-t; L=n-s
        c = h[s:]*(1 - h[:L])
        score_ones[s:] += c
        score_zeros[:L] += c
  # Choose candidate pool. We want a high score one and low? Actually b zero with high score means it is zero partner often; flipping b to one will reduce those contributions (good), but it may increase q where b is first and neighbor one. That is captured by full delta. High score_zero good. Choose top scores.
  ones_pool: top pool_size ones by score_ones, plus maybe random few.
  zeros_pool: top by score_zeros.
  Evaluate pairs:
    best_nm=curmax; best_ab=None; best_buf=None? We don't store buf.
    for a in pool_a:
      for b in pool_b:
         if a==b continue
         _fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n)
         q += buf
         nm=q.max()
         if nm < best_nm:
            best_nm=nm; best_a=a; best_b=b; best_buf=buf.copy() (or store delta)
         q -= buf
    if best_a is not None:
       q += best_buf; h[best_a]=0; h[best_b]=1; update lists; curmax=best_nm; best_h...
       restart score
    else:
       # No top-pair improves. Try random pairs (e.g., 2000) for improvement.
       found=False
       for r in random_count:
          a=rand one,b=rand zero; fill; q+=; nm=q.max(); if nm<curmax: apply; found True; break; else q-=
       if not found:
          # no improving move; break or perturb.
       break
But storing best_buf copy for each best improves cost; pool 30x30=900 copies length499 ~450k ints, okay maybe. We can avoid copy by if found best, after loop re-apply best candidate from scratch. Simpler: store best_a,b,best_nm. After loop, apply best (fill delta again) and q+=; h update. Good.

So q_new old [idx0 0, idx1 0? old t-2=0 +1=1? new t-2: i2 1*h0=0? 1*(1-0)=1 yes; idx2 t-1 old1+0=1; idx3 0; idx4 t1 2-1=1; idx5 t2 0+1=1; idx6 t3 1-1=0]. Max1. Correct! Great formula works.

Let's think about SLSQP gradient formula again with f from correlate. For t>0: f = ∑_{i=0}^{L-1} h_i - h_i h_{i+t}.
∂f/∂h_j:
- from A part: 1 if 0≤j<L.
- from -R: R=∑_{i=0}^{L-1} h_i h_{i+t}.
Derivative wrt h_j:
 if 0≤j<L: term h_{j+t} (as first)
 if t≤j<n: term h_{j-t} (as second)
So grad = (j<L) - (if j<L h_{j+t}) - (if j≥t h_{j-t}). Our code: gh[:L]+=w; gh[:L]-=w*h[t:]; gh[t:]-=w*h[:L]. For j<L, subtract h[j+t] yes. For j≥t, subtract h[j-t] via second slice aligned gh[t:] with h[:L]. Good.
For t<0, s=-t, L=n-s. f=∑_{i=s}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-s}) (because i+t=i-s). A part j in s..n-1. R=∑_{i=s}^{n-1} h_i h_{i-s}. Derivative:
 if j∈[s,n): h_{j-s} (first)
 if j-s ∈ [s,n)? as second? R term h_i h_{i-s}; if j=i-s -> i=j+s valid if j≤n-s-1? Let's use symmetry with t>0 but endpoints. Our code: gh[s:]+=w; gh[s:]-=w*h[:L]; gh[:L]-=w*h[s:]. For j≥s, subtract h[j-s] (since h[:L] aligned to gh[s:]) yes. For j<L (0..n-s-1), subtract h[s+j] (as second) yes. Good.
For t=0: f=∑h_i - h_i^2, grad 1-2h_i. Code gh += w; gh -= 2w*h. Good.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
 best_h=None; best_c=np.inf
 # initial
 try:
   h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
 except: n0=250; h0=np.full(n0,0.5)
 best_h, best_c = update(h0,n0)
 # Phase 1: SLSQP from initial projections (full, sym, anti) with alphas
 p1=t0+0.25*budget_s
 for mode in [0,1,2] if n0 even:
   param = get_param(mode)
   for alpha in [30, 200, 2000]:
     if time>p1: break
     param=slsqp(param, mode, alpha, maxiter=300, dl=min(p1, time+60))
     h=make_h(param,mode); update
 # Phase 2: binary ILS from best and random anti/sym? Use n0.
 p2=t0+0.55*budget_s
 hb, cb = binary_ilsearch_from_continuous(best_h,n0,p2,seed)
 update
 # also random anti n0 full binary:
 for r in range(3):
   if time<p2:
     h=rand_anti(n0,seed); binary_ilsearch h for limited 20s; update
 # Phase 3: continuous polish from best binary/best
 p3=t0+0.85*budget_s
 for mode/alpha from best:
   param project; slsqp; update
 # Phase 4: n_alt=256 if time
 if time<p3+... and budget enough:
   n_alt=256
   # build anti random and perhaps initialize from upsampled best? 
   for r in [anti random, sym random, upsampled best rebinarized]:
      h = make start n_alt
      # quick binary ILS limited 30s? maybe too much.
      # SLSQP anti/sym limited
      update
 # final polish: full SLSQP high alpha from best
 if time<deadline:
   h=best_h; for alpha in [5000,20000]: slsqp full maxiter 500; update
 # ensure feasible exact
 h_best=project_feasible(best_h,n_best)
 c_best=compute_c5(h_best,n_best)
 return h_best,c_best,n_best
```

def binary_il_search(h0,n,deadline,seed, outer_time=60,max_pool=24):
 h = h0.astype(np.int32)
 q = cint
 curmax=...
 best_h=h.copy(); best_q=curmax
 ones=zeros...
 rng=RandomState(seed)
 # Keep outer loop: descent until stuck, perturb, repeat.
 iter_time = deadline
 while time<deadline:
   # descent
   improved=True
   while time<deadline and improved:
      improved=False
      active=...
      # if active empty? break
      score...
      # pools
      a_pool = top ones + maybe random 5 ones
      b_pool = top zeros + random 5 zeros
      # Unique? no
      best_nm=curmax; best_a=-1
      buf=np.zeros(L,int32)
      for a in a_pool:
        for b in b_pool:
          if a==b: continue
          fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n)
          q += buf
          nm=int(q.max())
          if nm < best_nm:
             best_nm=nm; best_a=a; best_b=b
          q -= buf
      if best_a>=0:
         fill_delta(buf,h,best_a,best_b,n)
         q += buf
         # update lists: need find positions in lists. Instead of searching, we can maintain boolean and recompute lists after each accepted? n small; O(n) okay. Or update via lists search.
         h[best_a]=0; h[best_b]=1
         curmax=best_nm
         if curmax<best_q:
            best_q=curmax; best_h=h.copy()
         improved=True
         # recompute ones/zeros? We can update by swapping in lists if we know indices. Since we didn't track a/b positions, simpler recompute lists every accept:
         ones=[i for i,v in enumerate(h) if v==1]
         zeros=[i for i,v in enumerate(h) if v==0]
      else:
         # try random pairs 2000 for first improvement
         found=False
         for _ in range(2000):
            a=ones[rng.randrange(len(ones))]; b=zeros[rng.randrange(len(zeros))]
            fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n); q+=buf; nm=int(q.max())
            if nm<curmax:
              q? keep; apply; found=True; break
            q-=buf
         if found:
            apply; recompute; improved=True
         else:
            break
   if time>deadline: break
   if best_q? maybe if already target break
   # perturb if not at deadline
   for _ in range(rng.randint(2,7)):
      a=ones[rng.randrange(len(ones))]; b=zeros[rng.randrange(len(zeros))]
      fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n); q+=buf; h[a]=0;h[b]=1
   curmax=int(q.max())
   recompute lists; reset outer; maybe if curmax>best_q+3 perturb less?
```

Pseudo code final:
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

G_MODE=0; G_N=0; G_ALPHA=1.0; G_TARGET=0.0

def compute_c5(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float)
 dx=2.0/n
 c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
 return float(np.max(c)*dx)

def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); np.clip(h,0,1,out=h); target=n/2.0
 for _ in range(1000):
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
  if diff>0:
   room=1.0-h; s=room.sum()
   if s<1e-15: break
   h=np.clip(h + diff*(room/s),0,1)
  else:
   s=h.sum()
   if s<1e-15: break
   h=np.clip(h + diff*(h/s),0,1)
 return h

def project_param(x,target):
 x=np.asarray(x,float).copy(); np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
 for _ in range(1000):
  diff=target-x.sum(); if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
  if diff>0:
   room=1-x; s=room.sum(); ...
  else:
   s=x.sum(); ...
 return x

def f_counts(h):
 return np.correlate(np.asarray(h,float), 1.0-np.asarray(h,float), mode='full')

def make_h(x, mode, n):
 x=np.asarray(x,float)
 if mode==0: return x.copy()
 m=n//2
 h=np.empty(n,float)
 if mode==1:
   h[:m]=x; h[m:]=x[::-1]
 else:
   h[:m]=x; h[m:]=1.0-x
 return h

def get_param(h,mode,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float)
 if mode==0:
   return project_param(h,n/2.0)
 m=n//2
 if mode==1:
   x=0.5*(h[:m]+h[n-1:status?])
```
For h mirror: `h_mr = h[::-1][:m]`; x=0.5*(h[:m]+h_mr). But h[::-1] allocates length n; okay. Or `h[m? no`. For n even, first m mirrored by last m reversed: `h[n-m:]` reversed? If n=6,m=3: h indices 0 1 2 3 4 5; mirror of 0 is5,1->4,2->3. `h[m:][::-1]` gives [5,4,3]. So `x=0.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1])`. For n=2m, h[m:] length m. Good. Equivalent.
For anti: x=0.5*(h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m:]). Sum? yes.
Return x clipped; for sym project_param target=m/2=n/4; for anti no target but clip.
```
if mode==1: return project_param(x,m/2.0)
if mode==2: return np.clip(x,0,1)
```

`smooth_obj(x)`:
```
n=G_N; alpha=G_ALPHA
h=make_h(x,G_MODE,n)
f=f_counts(h)
m=np.max(f)
s=alpha*(f-m)
smax=np.max(s)
den=np.exp(s-smax).sum()
return float(m + (smax+np.log(den))/alpha)
```
`smooth_grad(x)`:
```
n=G_N; mode=G_MODE; alpha=G_ALPHA
h=make_h(x,mode,n)
f=f_counts(h)
m=np.max(f)
s=alpha*(f-m); smax=np.max(s)
p=np.exp(s-smax); p/=p.sum()
gh=np.zeros(n,float)
L=2*n-1; mid=n-1
thr=1e-10
for k in np.where(p>thr)[0]:
 wk=float(p[k]); t=k-mid
 if t==0: gh[:] += wk; gh -= 2*wk*h
 elif t>0:
   n_t=n-t
   gh[:n_t] += wk
   gh[:n_t] -= wk*h[t:]
   gh[t:] -= wk*h[:n_t]
 else:
   s=-t; n_t=n-s
   gh[s:] += wk
   gh[s:] -= wk*h[:n_t]
   gh[:n_t] -= wk*h[s:]
if mode==0: return gh
mvar=n//2
if mode==1:
 idx=np.arange(mvar)
 return gh[:mvar]+gh[n-1-idx]
else:
 return gh[:mvar]-gh[mvar:]
```
For mode1, h length n even. `gh[n-1-idx]` for idx 0..m-1: indices n-1..m. Good. If n even. For mode2, mirror derivative -1 for second half: h[m+i]=1-x_i. good.

Constraints:
```
def cons_fun(x):
 return float(np.sum(x)-G_TARGET)
def cons_jac(x):
 return np.ones_like(x)
```
SLSQP:
```
def slsqp_param(param, mode, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline):
 global G_MODE,G_N,G_ALPHA,G_TARGET
 G_MODE=mode; G_N=n; G_ALPHA=float(alpha); G_TARGET=n/2.0 if mode==0 else (n/4.0 if mode==1 else 0.0)
 m = n if mode==0 else n//2
 bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
 cons=[]
 if mode!=2:
   cons.append({'type':'eq','fun':cons_fun,'jac':cons_jac})
 try:
  res=minimize(smooth_obj, param, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'disp':0})
  x=res.x
 except:
  x=param
 if mode==0: x=project_param(x,n/2.0)
 elif mode==1: x=project_param(x,n/4.0)
 else: x=np.clip(x,0,1)
 return x
```
Potential: SLSQP may call cons_fun before setting G_TARGET? set before. For mode2 cons empty; cons_jac not used.

Need set G_TARGET for mode2 maybe 0. cons_fun not called if cons empty.

Potential issue: q is int32, `q >= curmax - 2` if curmax-2 negative includes all; active many. But curmax around 47. okay.

Let's draft code in analysis then final.

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

G_MODE=0; G_N=0; G_ALPHA=1.0; G_TARGET=0.0; G_PEN=0.0

def compute_c5(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=float(n)/2.0
    for _ in range(200):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; s=room.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h + diff*(room/s),0.0,1.0)
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h + diff*(h/s),0.0,1.0)
    return h

def project_param(x,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
    np.clip(x,0.0,1.0,out=x)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff=float(target)-x.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-x; s=room.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            x=np.clip(x + diff*(room/s),0.0,1.0)
        else:
            s=x.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            x=np.clip(x + diff*(x/s),0.0,1.0)
    return x

def f_counts(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    return np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')

def make_h(x,mode,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if mode==0:
        return x.copy()
    m=n//2
    h=np.empty(n,float)
    if mode==1:
        h[:m]=x; h[m:]=x[::-1]
    else:
        h[:m]=x; h[m:]=1.0-x
    return h

def get_param(h,mode,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if mode==0:
        return project_param(h,float(n)/2.0)
    m=n//2
    if mode==1:
        x=0.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1])
        return project_param(x,float(m)/2.0)
    x=0.5*(h[:m]+1.0-h[m:])
    return np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)

def smooth_objective(x):
    h=make_h(x,G_MODE,G_N)
    f=f_counts(h)
    m=f.max()
    s=G_ALPHA*(f-m)
    sm=s.max()
    return float(m + (sm + np.log(np.exp(s-sm).sum()))/G_ALPHA)

def smooth_gradient(x):
    h=make_h(x,G_MODE,G_N)
    f=f_counts(h)
    m=f.max()
    s=G_ALPHA*(f-m)
    sm=s.max()
    p=np.exp(s-sm); p /= p.sum()
    n=G_N; gh=np.zeros(n,float); mid=n-1
    inv=1e-10
    for k in np.where(p>inv)[0]:
        w=float(p[k]); t=int(k)-mid
        if t==0:
            gh += w
            gh -= 2.0*w*h
        elif t>0:
            L=n-t
            gh[:L] += w
            gh[:L] -= w*h[t:]
            gh[t:] -= w*h[:L]
        else:
            s=-t; L=n-s
            gh[s:] += w
            gh[s:] -= w*h[:L]
            gh[:L] -= w*h[s:]
    if G_MODE==0:
        return gh
    m=n//2
    if G_MODE==1:
        idx=np.arange(m)
        return gh[:m] + gh[n-1-idx]
    return gh[:m] - gh[m:]

def pen_objective(x):
    return smooth_objective(x) + G_PEN*(float(np.sum(x))-G_TARGET)**2

def pen_gradient(x):
    g=smooth_gradient(x)
    if G_PEN:
        g = g + (2.0*G_PEN*(float(np.sum(x))-G_TARGET))*np.ones_like(g)
    return g

def lbfgsb_param(param,mode,n,alpha,pen,maxiter):
    global G_MODE,G_N,G_ALPHA,G_TARGET,G_PEN
    G_MODE=mode; G_N=n; G_ALPHA=float(alpha); G_PEN=float(pen)
    if mode==0: m=n; target=float(n)/2.0
    elif mode==1: m=n//2; target=float(m)/2.0
    else: m=n//2; target=0.0
    G_TARGET=target
    param=np.asarray(param,float)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
    try:
        res=minimize(pen_objective, param, jac=pen_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B',
                     bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
        x=res.x
    except Exception:
        x=param
    if mode==0:
        x=project_param(x,target)
    elif mode==1:
        x=project_param(x,target)
    else:
        x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    return x

def maybe_update(h,n,best_h,best_c,best_n):
    if h is None or len(h)!=n:
        return best_h,best_c,best_n
    hh=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5(hh,n)
    if c < best_c:
        return hh,c,n
    return best_h,best_c,best_n

def rebinarize(h,n):
    if n%2:
        N=(n//2)
    else:
        N=n//2
    hb=np.zeros(n,np.int32)
    if N>0 and N<n:
        hb[np.argsort(h)[::-1][:N]]=1
    return hb

def continuous_optimize(base_h,n,deadline,best_h,best_c,best_n,modes,alphas,pens,maxiter):
    base_h=np.asarray(base_h,float)
    best_h,best_c,best_n=maybe_update(base_h,n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
    cur_h=base_h
    for mode in modes:
        if mode and n%2: continue
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
            p_list = [0.0] if mode==2 else pens
            for pen in p_list:
                if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
                param=get_param(cur_h,n,mode)
                mt=maxiter
                rem=deadline-time.time()
                if rem<10: mt=min(mt,50)
                param=lbfgsb_param(param,mode,n,alpha,pen,mt)
                hh=make_h(param,mode,n)
                best_h,best_c,best_n=maybe_update(hh,n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
                if best_n==n:
                    cur_h=best_h
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

 if do_binary and n%2==0 and time.time()<bin_dl:
   hb,cb=binary_il_search(start_h,n,bin_dl,seed)

def upsample_h(h,n):
 old=len(h)
 if old==n: return np.asarray(h,float)
 x_old=np.linspace(0,2.0,old,endpoint=False)
 x_new=np.linspace(0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
 return np.interp(x_new,x_old,np.asarray(h,float))

Let's verify `fill_delta` code line:
```
def fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n):
    buf[:] = 0
    st=n-1-a
    buf[st:st+n] += h - 1
    st=n-1-b
    buf[st:st+n] += 1 - h
    buf[st+a] += 1
    buf[st+b] -= 1
    buf[a:a+n][::-1] += h
    buf[n-1] -= 1
    buf[n-1+a-b] += 1
    buf[b:b+n][::-1] -= h
    buf[n-1+b-a] += 1
    buf[n-1+a-b] -= 1
```
Check first b slice: st=n-1-b. `st+a` index for neighbor j=a; valid if a 0..n-1. Good. `st+b`=n-1 (t=0). Special correction later may add to same; okay.

Potential if t>0, score_zeros[t:] length n-t=Lg. Good.

```
def binary_il_search(h0,n,deadline,seed):
    if n % 2:
        return None, None
    h = rebinarize(h0,n).astype(np.int32)
    q = np.rint(f_counts(h,n)).astype(np.int32)
    curmax = int(q.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_q = curmax
    L = 2*n - 1; mid = n-1
    buf = np.zeros(L, np.int32)
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        while time.time() < deadline:
            if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
            active = np.where(q >= curmax - 1)[0]
            sc1 = np.zeros(n, np.int32); sc0 = np.zeros(n, np.int32)
            for kk in active:
                t = int(kk) - mid
                if t == 0: continue
                if t > 0:
                    lg = n - t
                    c = h[:lg] * (1 - h[t:])
                    sc1[:lg] += c
                    sc0[t:] += c
                else:
                    s = -t; lg = n - s
                    c = h[s:] * (1 - h[:lg])
                    sc1[s:] += c
                    sc0[:lg] += c
            pool = 24
            if len(ones):
                a_idx = np.argsort(-sc1[ones])[:pool]
                a_set = [int(v) for v in ones[a_idx]]
            else: a_set=[]
            if len(zeros):
                b_idx = np.argsort(-sc0[zeros])[:pool]
                b_set = [int(v) for v in zeros[b_idx]]
            else: b_set=[]
            for _ in range(4):
                if len(ones): a_set.append(int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]))
                if len(zeros): b_set.append(int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]))
            a_set = list(dict.fromkeys(a_set)); b_set=list(dict.fromkeys(b_set))
            best_nm = curmax; ba = -1; bb = -1
            for a in a_set:
                for b in b_set:
                    if a == b: continue
                    fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n)
                    q += buf
                    nm = int(q.max())
                    if nm < best_nm:
                        best_nm=nm; ba=a; bb=b
                    q -= buf
            if ba >= 0:
                fill_delta(buf,h,ba,bb,n)
                q += buf
                h[ba] = 0; h[bb] = 1
                curmax = best_nm
                if curmax < best_q:
                    best_q = curmax; best_h = h.copy()
                ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1); zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
                continue
            found = False
            for _ in range(600):
                a = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                b = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n)
                q += buf
                nm = int(q.max())
                if nm < curmax:
                    h[a] = 0; h[b] = 1
                    curmax = nm
                    if curmax < best_q:
                        best_q = curmax; best_h = h.copy()
                    ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1); zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
                    found = True
                    break
                q -= buf
            if found:
                continue
            break
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        # perturb
        for _ in range(3):
            a = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            b = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
            fill_delta(buf,h,a,b,n)
            q += buf
            h[a] = 0; h[b] = 1
        curmax = int(q.max())
        ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1); zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
    cb = float(best_q) * (2.0 / n)
    return best_h.astype(float), cb
```

Now, think about if L-BFGS-B maxiter sets `maxiter` but not `maxcor`; default 10, okay. Objective nonconvex; more memory maybe not.

Now, final code in one block. Ensure no `status` lines. Let's produce final. But before final, let's review possible signs in gradient: For t>0, f=∑_{i=0}^{n-t-1} h_i - h_i h_{i+t}. `gh[t:] -= w*h[:L]` aligns index j=t..n-1 with h[j-t]. Good. For t<0, f=∑_{i=s}^{n-1} h_i - h_i h_{i-s}. `gh[s:] += w`; `gh[s:] -= w*h[:L]` aligns h[j-s]; `gh[:L] -= w*h[s:]` aligns j=0..L-1 with h[j+s]. Good.
For mode1 gradient transform: h[i]=x_i, h[n-1-i]=x_i. But our make_h `h[m:]=x[::-1]`: index m+i = x[m-1-i]? For n=2m, mirror of i is n-1-i = 2m-1-i. If i<m, mirror >=m. h[mirror] = h[m + (mirror-m)] = h[m + (m-1-i)] = x[m + (m-1-i) - m] = x[m-1-i]. x_i? Wait if h = [x0,x1,...,x_{m-1}, x_{m-1},...,x0]. Mirror of i is 2m-1-i; in second half position m + (m-1-i) value x_{m-1-i}. But for symmetric, h_i should equal h_{mirror} = x_{m-1-i}, not x_i. Did we define parameter wrong? For symmetric sequence of length 2m, if we set x length m as first half, second half should be reverse(x): h = [x0,x1,...,x_{m-1}, x_{m-1},...,x1,x0]. Then h_i (i=0) equals h_{2m-1}=x0? No, h_{2m-1} last = x0? In [x0,x1,...,x_{m-1},x_{m-1},...,x1,x0], mirror of 0 is last x0; good. Mirror of 1 is second last x1; yes. In general mirror of i is x_i. Our construction `h[:m]=x; h[m:]=x[::-1]` yields second half [x_{m-1},...,x_0]. Last = x_0. Mirror of i=0 -> index2m-1 -> x0; i=1 ->2m-2 -> x1. Good. My mapping `h[m + (m-1-i)] = x[m-1-i]`? For i=1, m-1-i=m-2 value x_{m-2}, but index 2m-2 is second half position m? Wait n=6,m=3: h=[x0,x1,x2,x2,x1,x0]. mirror of i=1 is4; h[4]=x1. formula m-1-i=1? m-1=2, 2-1=1 -> x1. I mistakenly said m-1-i. Good. So gradient transform `gh[:m]+gh[n-1-idx]` correct.
`get_param` mode1: x=0.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1]). For h sym, h[m:][::-1] = [x0,x1,...,x_{m-1}], so x recovered. Good.

For anti mode: h=[x,1-x]. Gradient transform `gh[:m] - gh[m:]`. Since h[m+i]=1-x_i, derivative -1. Good.
`get_param` anti x=0.5*(h[:m]+1-h[m:]) recovers if anti. Good.

Now, note for anti h total sum = m, target n/2=m. For n=250 m=125. Good.

Potential issue: For anti binary, rebinarize from continuous h may break anti; but binary ILS full okay. continuous mode anti from h uses get_param projection. Good.

Now, maybe continuous optimization from anti mode with L-BFGS can find values not binary; final full project preserves sum but may break anti. Good.

Let's consider if final best from continuous anti but project_feasible slightly breaks anti and increases C; unavoidable. Could we return the anti constructed h without full project? It already sum exact (within floating). We could if sum error tiny return h as is to keep property. `project_feasible` may change values away from anti. To avoid, in `maybe_update`, project_feasible for update. For ant starts, h sum exact; project diff maybe tiny and changes negligibly. Fine.

Could we in final if best came from anti mode store a flag? Not. No need.

Now, check `compute_c5` for n maybe 0? no.

Let's now produce final code. Ensure no use of pdb etc.

One more thought: The record 0.38085 target 0.3808. Our algorithm might not run to completion if L-BFGS slow. But leaves best parent if SLSQB overruns. Parent from previous program 0.380865; target 0.3808. Need improvement. If our continuous L-BFGS from parent with gradient actually polishes below 0.3808, great. If not, binary may. We hope.

Could we add a very simple deterministic fine-tuning: Take best h, active max lags, average values? No.

Let's ensure alpha values: For final, if alpha=6000 and f differences ~0.1 counts, exp(-600) zero, only exact max lags active. If max lags tied and gradient sum zero, no move. Maybe alpha=1000 better to include near active. Use final alphas (2000, 10000)? If first alpha 2000 can move, second 10000 polish. In continuous_optimize, alphas sequence used. Let's ensure maxiter in continuous_optimize for final with rem final ~200s, mt 350; each solve maybe 350 iter; 4 solves =1400 iter. Could be okay. If slow, time runs out after some; final phase has deadline final_dl, after it not checked? It runs until solves. If a solve exceeds final_dl by maxiter, program could be killed. If L-BFGS B 250 vars maybe 1 iter 0.05s? 17s per solve. 4=68s. okay. If 0.5s, 175s per solve -> bad. Hard.

</think>

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

G_MODE = 0
G_N = 0
G_ALPHA = 1.0
G_TARGET = 0.0
G_PEN = 0.0


def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float).copy()
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    for _ in range(300):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + diff * (room / s), 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + diff * (h / s), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def project_param(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, float).copy()
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    target = float(target)
    for _ in range(300):
        diff = target - x.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            x = np.clip(x + diff * (room / s), 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = x.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            x = np.clip(x + diff * (x / s), 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def f_counts(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def make_h(x, mode, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    if mode == 0:
        return x.copy()
    m = n // 2
    h = np.empty(n, float)
    if mode == 1:
        h[:m] = x
        h[m:] = x[::-1]
    else:
        h[:m] = x
        h[m:] = 1.0 - x
    return h


def get_param(h, mode, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    if mode == 0:
        return project_param(h, float(n) / 2.0)
    m = n // 2
    if mode == 1:
        x = 0.5 * (h[:m] + h[m:][::-1])
        return project_param(x, float(m) / 2.0)
    x = 0.5 * (h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m:])
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)


def smooth_objective(x):
    h = make_h(x, G_MODE, G_N)
    f = f_counts(h)
    m = f.max()
    s = G_ALPHA * (f - m)
    sm = s.max()
    return float(m + (sm + np.log(np.exp(s - sm).sum())) / G_ALPHA)


def smooth_gradient(x):
    h = make_h(x, G_MODE, G_N)
    f = f_counts(h)
    m = f.max()
    s = G_ALPHA * (f - m)
    sm = s.max()
    p = np.exp(s - sm)
    p /= p.sum()

    n = G_N
    gh = np.zeros(n, float)
    mid = n - 1
    thr = 1e-10

    for k in np.where(p > thr)[0]:
        w = float(p[k])
        t = int(k) - mid
        if t == 0:
            gh += w
            gh -= 2.0 * w * h
        elif t > 0:
            L = n - t
            gh[:L] += w
            gh[:L] -= w * h[t:]
            gh[t:] -= w * h[:L]
        else:
            s = -t
            L = n - s
            gh[s:] += w
            gh[s:] -= w * h[:L]
            gh[:L] -= w * h[s:]

    if G_MODE == 0:
        return gh
    m = n // 2
    if G_MODE == 1:
        idx = np.arange(m)
        return gh[:m] + gh[n - 1 - idx]
    return gh[:m] - gh[m:]


def pen_objective(x):
    return smooth_objective(x) + G_PEN * (float(np.sum(x)) - G_TARGET) ** 2


def pen_gradient(x):
    g = smooth_gradient(x)
    if G_PEN:
        g = g + (2.0 * G_PEN * (float(np.sum(x)) - G_TARGET)) * np.ones_like(g)
    return g


def lbfgsb_param(param, mode, n, alpha, pen, maxiter):
    global G_MODE, G_N, G_ALPHA, G_TARGET, G_PEN
    G_MODE = mode
    G_N = n
    G_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    G_PEN = float(pen)
    if mode == 0:
        m = n
        target = float(n) / 2.0
    elif mode == 1:
        m = n // 2
        target = float(m) / 2.0
    else:
        m = n // 2
        target = 0.0
    G_TARGET = target

    param = np.asarray(param, float)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    try:
        res = minimize(pen_objective, param, jac=pen_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B',
                       bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-8})
        x = res.x
    except Exception:
        x = param

    if mode == 0 or mode == 1:
        x = project_param(x, target)
    else:
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def maybe_update(h, n, best_h, best_c, best_n):
    if h is None or len(h) != n:
        return best_h, best_c, best_n
    hh = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(hh, n)
    if c < best_c:
        return hh, c, n
    return best_h, best_c, best_n


def rebinarize(h, n):
    N = n // 2
    hb = np.zeros(n, np.int32)
    if 0 < N < n:
        hb[np.argsort(h)[::-1][:N]] = 1
    return hb


def continuous_optimize(base_h, n, deadline, best_h, best_c, best_n,
                        modes, alphas, pens, maxiter):
    base_h = np.asarray(base_h, float)
    best_h, best_c, best_n = maybe_update(base_h, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
    cur_h = base_h
    for mode in modes:
        if mode and (n % 2):
            continue
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 2:
                break
            pen_list = [0.0] if mode == 2 else list(pens)
            for pen in pen_list:
                if time.time() > deadline - 2:
                    break
                rem = deadline - time.time()
                if rem < 8:
                    break
                mt = maxiter
                if rem < 25:
                    mt = min(mt, 50)
                elif rem < 60:
                    mt = min(mt, 120)
                param = get_param(cur_h, n, mode)
                param = lbfgsb_param(param, mode, n, alpha, pen, mt)
                hh = make_h(param, mode, n)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = maybe_update(hh, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
                if best_n == n:
                    cur_h = best_h
    return best_h, best_c, best_n


def fill_delta(buf, h, a, b, n):
    buf[:] = 0
    st = n - 1 - a
    buf[st:st + n] += h - 1

    st = n - 1 - b
    buf[st:st + n] += 1 - h
    buf[st + a] += 1
    buf[st + b] -= 1

    buf[a:a + n][::-1] += h
    buf[n - 1] -= 1
    buf[n - 1 + a - b] += 1

    buf[b:b + n][::-1] -= h

    buf[n - 1 + b - a] += 1
    buf[n - 1 + a - b] -= 1


def binary_il_search(h0, n, deadline, seed):
    if n % 2:
        return None, None
    h = rebinarize(h0, n).astype(np.int32)
    q = np.rint(f_counts(h, n)).astype(np.int32)
    curmax = int(q.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_q = curmax
    L = 2 * n - 1
    mid = n - 1
    buf = np.zeros(L, np.int32)
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    while time.time() < deadline:
        while time.time() < deadline:
            if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
                break

            active = np.where(q >= curmax - 1)[0]
            sc1 = np.zeros(n, np.int32)
            sc0 = np.zeros(n, np.int32)
            for kk in active:
                t = int(kk) - mid
                if t == 0:
                    continue
                if t > 0:
                    Lg = n - t
                    c = h[:Lg] * (1 - h[t:])
                    sc1[:Lg] += c
                    sc0[t:] += c
                else:
                    s = -t
                    Lg = n - s
                    c = h[s:] * (1 - h[:Lg])
                    sc1[s:] += c
                    sc0[:Lg] += c

            pool = 24
            if len(ones):
                a_idx = np.argsort(-sc1[ones])[:pool]
                a_set = [int(v) for v in ones[a_idx]]
            else:
                a_set = []
            if len(zeros):
                b_idx = np.argsort(-sc0[zeros])[:pool]
                b_set = [int(v) for v in zeros[b_idx]]
            else:
                b_set = []

            for _ in range(4):
                if len(ones):
                    a_set.append(int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]))
                if len(zeros):
                    b_set.append(int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]))
            a_set = list(dict.fromkeys(a_set))
            b_set = list(dict.fromkeys(b_set))

            best_nm = curmax
            ba = -1
            bb = -1
            for a in a_set:
                for b in b_set:
                    if a == b:
                        continue
                    fill_delta(buf, h, a, b, n)
                    q += buf
                    nm = int(q.max())
                    if nm < best_nm:
                        best_nm = nm
                        ba = a
                        bb = b
                    q -= buf

            if ba >= 0:
                fill_delta(buf, h, ba, bb, n)
                q += buf
                h[ba] = 0
                h[bb] = 1
                curmax = best_nm
                if curmax < best_q:
                    best_q = curmax
                    best_h = h.copy()
                ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
                zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
                continue

            found = False
            for _ in range(600):
                a = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                b = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                fill_delta(buf, h, a, b, n)
                q += buf
                nm = int(q.max())
                if nm < curmax:
                    h[a] = 0
                    h[b] = 1
                    curmax = nm
                    if curmax < best_q:
                        best_q = curmax
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
                    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
                    found = True
                    break
                q -= buf

            if found:
                continue
            break

        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        for _ in range(3):
            a = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            b = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
            fill_delta(buf, h, a, b, n)
            q += buf
            h[a] = 0
            h[b] = 1

        curmax = int(q.max())
        ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)

    cb = float(best_q) * (2.0 / n)
    return best_h.astype(float), cb


def upsample_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    old = len(h)
    if old == n:
        return h.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)


def make_anti_tm(n):
    m = n // 2
    x = np.zeros(m, np.int32)
    for i in range(1, m):
        x[i] = x[i >> 1] ^ (i & 1)
    return make_h(x.astype(float), 2, n)


def random_anti_h(n, seed):
    m = n // 2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = (rng.rand(m) < 0.5).astype(float)
    return make_h(x, 2, n)


def trial(start_h, n, seed, bin_dl, cont_dl, best_h, best_c, best_n,
          modes, alphas, pens, maxiter, do_binary=True):
    if do_binary and (n % 2 == 0) and time.time() < bin_dl:
        hb, cb = binary_il_search(start_h, n, bin_dl, seed)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = maybe_update(hb, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
    if time.time() < cont_dl:
        base = best_h if best_n == n else start_h
        best_h, best_c, best_n = continuous_optimize(
            base, n, cont_dl, best_h, best_c, best_n, modes, alphas, pens, maxiter)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    T = float(budget_s)
    final_dl = t0 + max(60.0, T - 8.0)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = make_anti_tm(n0)

    best_h, best_c, best_n = maybe_update(h0, n0, None, np.inf, 0)
    even0 = (best_n % 2 == 0)
    modes_even = [2, 0, 1]
    modes_odd = [0]

    p1 = min(t0 + 0.20 * T, final_dl - 80.0)
    best_h, best_c, best_n = continuous_optimize(
        best_h, best_n, p1, best_h, best_c, best_n,
        modes_even if even0 else modes_odd,
        (100.0, 1000.0, 4000.0), (1000.0,), 120)

    if even0:
        p2 = min(t0 + 0.32 * T, final_dl - 50.0)
        if time.time() < p2:
            hb, cb = binary_il_search(best_h, best_n, p2, seed)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = maybe_update(hb, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

        p3 = min(t0 + 0.39 * T, final_dl - 40.0)
        if time.time() < p3:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = continuous_optimize(
                best_h, best_n, p3, best_h, best_c, best_n,
                modes_even, (2500.0,), (5000.0,), 160)

        p4b = min(t0 + 0.43 * T, p3 + 12.0)
        p4c = min(t0 + 0.48 * T, final_dl - 20.0)
        start_tm = make_anti_tm(best_n)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = trial(
            start_tm, best_n, seed + 1, p4b, p4c, best_h, best_c, best_n,
            (2, 0), (800.0,), (1000.0,), 110, True)

        p5b = min(t0 + 0.52 * T, p4c + 8.0)
        p5c = min(t0 + 0.56 * T, final_dl - 20.0)
        start_rand = random_anti_h(best_n, seed + 2)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = trial(
            start_rand, best_n, seed + 3, p5b, p5c, best_h, best_c, best_n,
            (2,), (1200.0,), (1000.0,), 100, True)

    na = 256
    p6b = min(t0 + 0.66 * T, final_dl - 150.0)
    p6c = min(t0 + 0.79 * T, final_dl - 70.0)
    if T > 700.0 and time.time() < p6c:
        src = upsample_h(best_h, na)
        start_alt = rebinarize(src, na).astype(float)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = trial(
            start_alt, na, seed + 4, p6b, p6c, best_h, best_c, best_n,
            (2, 0, 1), (600.0, 2500.0), (1000.0,), 150, True)

        p7b = min(t0 + 0.83 * T, p6c + 10.0)
        p7c = min(t0 + 0.88 * T, final_dl - 20.0)
        if time.time() < p7c:
            start_alt2 = random_anti_h(na, seed + 5)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = trial(
                start_alt2, na, seed + 6, p7b, p7c, best_h, best_c, best_n,
                (2, 0), (2500.0,), (1000.0,), 120, True)

    fmodes = [2, 0] if (best_n % 2 == 0) else [0]
    best_h, best_c, best_n = continuous_optimize(
        best_h, best_n, final_dl, best_h, best_c, best_n,
        fmodes, (3500.0, 12000.0), (20000.0,), 220)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
